# Pilots' Quarter: buildings and rooms

Deck 5 · Pilots' Quarter. Every building in the district, floor by floor, with every room and what it does (Jordan, 27 Nov 2026: "I want to know what each room does").

**197 buildings, 224,986 rooms.**

This doc is written from the data by `Invicta/tools/rooms_doc.py`; change the building's room program (`Invicta/tools/room_programs/`) or its plan (`Invicta/data/models/`), then re-run it. Rooms with the same name on a floor are counted together; floors that are exactly alike are shown once. Room plans are **proposed** unless a building says otherwise. Sizes are US first, metric in brackets.

## P1 · Flight School

Flight school, training hangar, trainee halls

### P1-01 · Flight School Main Hall

**What it is for:** The heart of Aerion's flight school, with classrooms, lecture theatres and the school offices where trainee pilots learn the theory of flying.

58 ft (17.6 m) tall, 4 above deck, 5,744,700 sq ft (533,700 m²) of rooms. Room plan proposed.

**Look:** A broad teal-trimmed school with a tall glass entrance hall and the flight school's wings badge above its doors.

#### Level 1 · Ground floor

Floor at deck level; 16 ft (5 m) floor to floor.

| Room | How many | Floor area | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| Great Hall | 1 | 166,800 sq ft (15,500 m²) | The school's assembly hall, used for lectures, assemblies and the welcome of each new intake. |
| Entrance hall | 1 | 4,320 sq ft (401 m²) | The school's grand entrance, hung with photos of every graduating class. |
| Admissions office | 1 | 860 sq ft (80 m²) | New trainees enrol here. |
| Lecture theatre | 2 | 3,230 sq ft (300 m²) each | Lectures for a whole intake of trainees. |
| Toilets | 1 | 650 sq ft (60 m²) | Public toilets, with an accessible toilet and baby-changing room. |
| Classroom | 396 | 2,250 sq ft (209 m²) each | A classroom for 24 trainees, with screens and a cockpit diagram wall. |
| Study room | 395 | 970 sq ft (90 m²) each | Trainees revise together in small groups. |

| Room | How many | Floor area | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| Staff room | 1 | 1,290 sq ft (120 m²) | Instructors' lounge and kitchen. |
| Tea point | 1 | 370 sq ft (35 m²) | A small kitchen with a sink, fridge and drinks machine for staff on this floor. |
| Toilets | 1 | 640 sq ft (60 m²) | Public toilets, with an accessible toilet and baby-changing room. |
| Classroom | 232 | 2,260 sq ft (210 m²) each | A classroom for 24 trainees, with screens and a cockpit diagram wall. |
| Instructors' office | 232 | 1,320 sq ft (122 m²) each | Desks for flight instructors. |
| Exam room | 232 | 2,580 sq ft (240 m²) each | Written and screen exams. |

### P1-03 · Training Flight Line

**What it is for:** The line where training craft are parked, fuelled and serviced between lessons, and where trainees start and finish their training flights.

26 ft (8 m) tall, open apron; a line hut, 2,150 sq ft (200 m²) of rooms. Room plan proposed.

**Look:** Rows of small white-and-teal training craft on a floodlit apron, with fuel bowsers moving between them.

Floor at deck level; 13 ft (4 m) floor to floor.

| Room | How many | Floor area | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| Flight line hut | 1 | 1,430 sq ft (133 m²) | Line staff sign craft out and in. |
| Trainees' shelter | 1 | 720 sq ft (67 m²) | Trainees wait for their craft here. |
| Craft parking (open air) | 1 | 1,658,100 sq ft (154,050 m²) | Training craft parked in rows, chocked and tied down, between lessons. |
| Fuelling apron (open air) | 1 | 414,600 sq ft (38,520 m²) | Fuel bowsers and power carts service craft between flights. |
| Start-up line (open air) | 1 | 414,600 sq ft (38,520 m²) | Where trainees start their engines and run final checks before taxiing out. |
| Taxi lane (open air) | 1 | 414,600 sq ft (38,520 m²) | The lane craft taxi along to and from the flight deck. |

### P3-20 · Commons Service Shaft

**What it is for:** The Commons' shaft down to the service layer.

49 ft (15 m) tall, 2 above deck, 1 below, 638,200 sq ft (59,290 m²) of rooms. Room plan proposed.

**Look:** A squat concrete drum with a ring of yellow hazard lights round the lift doors and a steel gantry crane on its roof.

#### Level B1 · Service layer landing

Floor at 20 ft (6 m) below the deck; 20 ft (6 m) floor to floor.

| Room | How many | Floor area | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| Service layer landing | 1 | 153,400 sq ft (14,260 m²) | The foot of the shaft in the service layer, where tunnels lead off to the precinct's pipes, cables and plant. |
| Tunnel portal | 1 | 153,400 sq ft (14,260 m²) | Where the service tunnels meet the shaft. |

#### Level 1 · Lift landing

Floor at deck level; 30 ft (9 m) floor to floor.

| Room | How many | Floor area | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| Shaft and lift landing | 1 | 270,600 sq ft (25,140 m²) | The top of the shaft that drops 197 ft (60 m) to the service layer: goods lifts, a crew lift and a stair land here. Inside: Goods lift cage, Crew lift and stair. |
| Lift control room | 1 | 540 sq ft (50 m²) | Operators run the goods lifts and watch the shaft cameras from here. |
| Loading bay | 1 | 1,630 sq ft (151 m²) | Trucks back in here to load and unload goods going to or from the service layer. |
| Crew check-in | 1 | 430 sq ft (40 m²) | Crews going down sign out, pick up radios and gas detectors and sign back in when they return. |
| Tool store | 1 | 860 sq ft (80 m²) | Tools, harnesses and lamps for work in the service layer. |
| Staff toilets and showers | 1 | 750 sq ft (69 m²) | Toilets, showers and changing space for the staff. |
| Holding store | 18 | 1,450 sq ft (135 m²) each | Short-term storage for goods waiting for the lift. |

#### Level 2 · Winding room

| Room | How many | Floor area | What it does |
|---|---|---|---|
| Shaft and lift landing | 1 | 270,600 sq ft (25,140 m²) | The top of the shaft that drops 197 ft (60 m) to the service layer: goods lifts, a crew lift and a stair land here. Inside: Goods lift cage, Crew lift and stair. |
| Winding machinery | 1 | 2,170 sq ft (202 m²) | The motors, brakes and cables that drive the shaft lifts. |
| Shaft ventilation fans | 1 | 1,290 sq ft (119 m²) | Fans that push fresh air down the shaft and the service tunnels. |

| Control room | 1 | 1,290 sq ft (120 m²) | Plant operators watch power, air and water for the whole precinct on screens here, round the clock. |
| Switch room | 1 | 1,330 sq ft (124 m²) | Rows of switchgear that route power to each street and building, and cut it off when there is a fault. |
| Battery room | 1 | 1,720 sq ft (159 m²) | Banks of batteries that keep the precinct's lights and controls on for hours if the power fails. |
| Water pump room | 1 | 2,150 sq ft (199 m²) | Pumps that keep the precinct's fresh water at pressure and push waste water down to the service layer. |
| Water treatment room | 1 | 1,370 sq ft (128 m²) | Filters and UV lamps that clean recycled water before it goes back into the pipes. |
| Workshop | 1 | 1,500 sq ft (139 m²) | Benches and tools where the plant crew repair pumps, valves and fans. |
| Locker room | 1 | 970 sq ft (90 m²) | Lockers and changing benches for staff arriving for their shift. |
| Staff toilets and showers | 1 | 750 sq ft (70 m²) | Toilets, showers and changing space for the staff. |
| Spares store | 16 | 1,270 sq ft (118 m²) each | Racked spare pumps, valves, filters and fuses, so repairs never wait for parts. |
